Default streaming follow-up, 2026-10-11

Trailing whitespace is trimmed for Git hygiene; sample fields are unchanged.

Merged production base: 0a5969ed1e13eba7c30cdf2478dc8ce39c180e37
Original baseline: Phase 0 36a8e6f, production unchanged from 9623228.
No production changes in this follow-up. The phase harness is added identically
to baseline and merged facades; removing it reproduces both frozen binaries
byte for byte. Same generated guest and Wasm; build and source hashes below.
Both facade proofs pass their tests and the 1x phase-oracle run before sampling.
Initial GOMAXPROCS16, actual P1 asserted in each leaf; CGO0, Go1.26.8,
PGOoff, GOAMD64v1, default GC/memory, no affinity, Ryzen75800HS.
No build/lint/test/fuzz/reviewer tools overlap timed samples.
New/Close is one lifetime; Writes use primed 1024B x windows, with or without
prior injection; End and Close groups contain32 exports per ns/op.
Group preparation, oracles and ownership probes are untimed.
Cleanup follows timed End; abandoned Close times disposal directly.
Memory growth for groups spans the entire diagnostic, including untimed work.
These controls cannot be added/subtracted as a full-stream cost model.
Newly linked full-stream signs are diagnostics, not production gains.


===== reproduce-frozen.py =====

from pathlib import Path
import os, subprocess, datetime, hashlib, re, time
root=Path.cwd(); out=root/'.cache/default-streaming'; start=time.monotonic()
env=dict(os.environ,GOTOOLCHAIN='local',CGO_ENABLED='0',GOAMD64='v1',GOMAXPROCS='16')
binaries={'baseline':root/'.cache/sessions-phase0/stream-baseline-r12.test','merged':root/'.cache/sessions-phase0/stream-r12.test'}
sha=lambda path:hashlib.sha256(Path(path).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
print('UTC',datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat(),flush=True)
print('merged base',subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],text=True).strip(),flush=True)
print('Frozen R12 and original binaries; Go1.26.8 CGO0 PGOoff GOAMD64v1; default GC/memory; no affinity; no builds/tests/lint/fuzz/agent tools during timing',flush=True)
print('affinity',sorted(os.sched_getaffinity(0)),flush=True)
for key in ['GOGC','GOMEMLIMIT','GODEBUG']: print(key,env.get(key,'<unset>'),flush=True)
for label,binary in binaries.items():
 print(label,'SHA256',sha(binary),flush=True)
 print(subprocess.check_output(['/usr/bin/go','version','-m',str(binary)],text=True),flush=True)
base=Path('/tmp/cocoon-sessions-phase0-sy_birrp')
for name in ['testdata/streaming/go/streaming/testdata/module.wasm','testdata/streaming/go/streaming/internal/wasm/module.go','testdata/streaming/go/streaming/standalone_bench_test.go']:
 assert sha(root/name)==sha(base/name)
 print('identical-source',name,sha(root/name),flush=True)
patterns={'screen':'^BenchmarkStreamStandalone$/^(Fixed|Holdout)$/^P1$/^Chunk(64|4096)$/^Retain32$','confirm':'^BenchmarkStreamStandalone$/^(Fixed|Holdout)$/^P1$/^Chunk64$/^Retain32$'}
for block,duration,expected in [('screen','700ms',4),('confirm','1s',2)]:
 for rep in range(1,8):
  first=['baseline','merged'] if rep%2 else ['merged','baseline']
  if block=='confirm': first=first[::-1]
  for label in first:
   args=[str(binaries[label]),'-test.run=^$','-test.bench='+patterns[block],'-test.benchtime='+duration,'-test.count=1','-test.benchmem']
   path=out/f'frozen-{block}-{label}-{rep}.txt'
   with path.open('w') as log:
    log.write('# UTC '+datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat()+'\n# '+' '.join(args)+'\n# InitialGOMAXPROCS16; every measured leaf sets P1 and asserts actual-procs\n');log.flush()
    subprocess.run(args,env=env,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,check=True)
   rows=[line for line in path.read_text().splitlines() if line.startswith('Benchmark') and 'ns/op' in line]
   assert len(rows)==expected,(path,len(rows))
   for row in rows:
    fields=row.split()
    assert '/P1/' in fields[0] and 'actual-procs' in fields and float(fields[fields.index('actual-procs')-1])==1,row
   print(path.name,'completed',round(time.monotonic()-start,1),'s',flush=True)
 print(block,'COMPLETE',flush=True)
 for label in binaries:
  (out/f'frozen-{block}-{label}-combined.txt').write_text('\n'.join((out/f'frozen-{block}-{label}-{rep}.txt').read_text() for rep in range(1,8)))
 with (out/f'frozen-{block}-benchstat.txt').open('w') as log:
  subprocess.run(['/home/dario/go/bin/benchstat',str(out/f'frozen-{block}-baseline-combined.txt'),str(out/f'frozen-{block}-merged-combined.txt')],stdout=log,check=True)
print('FROZEN REPRODUCTION COMPLETE',round(time.monotonic()-start,1),'s',flush=True)

===== measure-phases.py =====

from pathlib import Path
import os, subprocess, datetime, hashlib, re, time
root=Path.cwd(); out=root/'.cache/default-streaming'; start=time.monotonic()
env=dict(os.environ,GOTOOLCHAIN='local',CGO_ENABLED='0',GOAMD64='v1',GOMAXPROCS='16')
binaries={'baseline':out/'phase-baseline.test','merged':out/'phase-merged.test'}
sha=lambda path:hashlib.sha256(Path(path).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
print('UTC',datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat(),flush=True)
print('merged base',subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],text=True).strip(),flush=True)
print('Identical diagnostic source added to original and merged facades; Go1.26.8 CGO0 PGOoff GOAMD64v1; default GC/memory; no affinity; no builds/tests/lint/fuzz/agent tools during timing',flush=True)
print('affinity',sorted(os.sched_getaffinity(0)),flush=True)
for key in ['GOGC','GOMEMLIMIT','GODEBUG']: print(key,env.get(key,'<unset>'),flush=True)
for label,binary in binaries.items():
 print(label,'SHA256',sha(binary),flush=True)
 print(subprocess.check_output(['/usr/bin/go','version','-m',str(binary)],text=True),flush=True)
base=Path('/tmp/cocoon-sessions-phase0-sy_birrp')
for name in ['testdata/streaming/go/streaming/testdata/module.wasm','testdata/streaming/go/streaming/internal/wasm/module.go','testdata/streaming/go/streaming/standalone_bench_test.go','testdata/streaming/go/streaming/phase_bench_test.go']:
 assert sha(root/name)==sha(base/name)
 print('identical-source',name,sha(root/name),flush=True)
patterns={'oracle':'^BenchmarkStreamPhases$', 'phases':'^BenchmarkStreamPhases$', 'full':'^BenchmarkStreamStandalone$/^(Fixed|Holdout)$/^P1$/^Chunk(64|4096)$/^Retain32$', 'frozen-post':'^BenchmarkStreamStandalone$/^(Fixed|Holdout)$/^P1$/^Chunk64$/^Retain32$'}
for block,duration,expected,repeats in [('oracle','1x',14,1),('phases','250ms',14,7),('full','700ms',4,7),('frozen-post','1s',2,7)]:
 if block=='frozen-post':
  binaries={'baseline':root/'.cache/sessions-phase0/stream-baseline-r12.test','merged':root/'.cache/sessions-phase0/stream-r12.test'}
 for rep in range(1,repeats+1):
  first=['baseline','merged'] if rep%2 else ['merged','baseline']
  if block in ['full','frozen-post']: first=first[::-1]
  for label in first:
   args=[str(binaries[label]),'-test.run=^$','-test.bench='+patterns[block],'-test.benchtime='+duration,'-test.count=1','-test.benchmem']
   path=out/f'diagnostic-{block}-{label}-{rep}.txt'
   with path.open('w') as log:
    log.write('# UTC '+datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat()+'\n# '+' '.join(args)+'\n# InitialGOMAXPROCS16; every measured leaf sets P1 and asserts actual-procs\n');log.flush()
    subprocess.run(args,env=env,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,check=True)
   rows=[line for line in path.read_text().splitlines() if line.startswith('Benchmark') and 'ns/op' in line]
   assert len(rows)==expected,(path,len(rows))
   for row in rows:
    fields=row.split()
    assert '/P1/' in fields[0] and 'actual-procs' in fields and float(fields[fields.index('actual-procs')-1])==1,row
    if block in ['oracle','phases']:
     units=[unit for unit in ['writes/op','new-close/op','ends/op','closes/op'] if unit in fields]
     assert len(units)==1,row
     count=float(fields[fields.index(units[0])-1])
     assert count==(32 if units[0] in ['ends/op','closes/op'] else 1),row
     assert float(fields[fields.index('guest-growth-B')-1])==0,row
   print(path.name,'completed',round(time.monotonic()-start,1),'s',flush=True)
 print(block,'COMPLETE',flush=True)
 for label in binaries:
  (out/f'diagnostic-{block}-{label}-combined.txt').write_text('\n'.join((out/f'diagnostic-{block}-{label}-{rep}.txt').read_text() for rep in range(1,repeats+1)))
 with (out/f'diagnostic-{block}-benchstat.txt').open('w') as log:
  subprocess.run(['/home/dario/go/bin/benchstat',str(out/f'diagnostic-{block}-baseline-combined.txt'),str(out/f'diagnostic-{block}-merged-combined.txt')],stdout=log,check=True)
print('DIAGNOSTIC RUN COMPLETE',round(time.monotonic()-start,1),'s',flush=True)

===== compare-assembly.py =====

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Compare existing Go amd64 binaries without building or executing them.

Run from the Cocoon root. Defaults compare the original frozen streaming
baseline with the retained R12 / merged implementation. Override --left,
--right, --out to compare the binaries containing the phase benchmark source.

Normalization drops source locations, instruction addresses, and encoded bytes;
direct local branch targets become instruction indices; unnamed IP-relative
data relocation displacements become <data>(IP). Named SB symbols stay intact.
Registers, stack/member offsets, ordinary immediates, instruction order, local
branch destinations, named callees/data symbols, and nm byte sizes remain.
Equal normalized output is not proof of equivalent referenced data, placement,
instruction encoding, compiler metadata, allocation layout, or performance.
Raw nm/objdump output and SHA256 provenance are saved beside the comparison.
"""

import argparse
import datetime
import difflib
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import subprocess
import sys

RT = "dario.cat/cocoon/rt"
STREAM = "dario.cat/cocoon/testdata/streaming/go/streaming"
ROOTS = {
    RT + ".(*Instance).Call": "Instance.Call",
    RT + ".(*Call).ResultView": "Call.ResultView",
    STREAM + ".(*Stream).Write": "Stream.Write",
    STREAM + ".(*Stream).End": "Stream.End",
    STREAM + ".(*Stream).Close": "Stream.Close",
    STREAM + ".(*Library).NewStream": "Library.NewStream",
    STREAM + ".(*Library).getHome": "Library.getHome",
    STREAM + ".(*Library).selectHome": "Library.selectHome",
    STREAM + ".streamBenchStandalone": "streamBenchStandalone",
    STREAM + "/internal/wasm.(*Module).Xcocoon_stream_write": "guest.Write",
}
INSN = re.compile(r"^\s+\S+:\d+\s+(0x[0-9a-f]+)\s+([0-9a-f]+)\s+(.+?)\s*$")


def capture(*args):
    return subprocess.check_output(args, text=True)


def sha256(path):
    with path.open("rb") as source:
        return hashlib.file_digest(source, "sha256").hexdigest()


def role(name):
    for root, label in ROOTS.items():
        if name == root or name.startswith(root + "."):
            return label + name[len(root):]
    if name.startswith(RT + ".(*Resource[") and STREAM + ".Stream" in name:
        match = re.search(r"\]\)\.(Use|Close)(.*)$", name)
        if match:
            shape = "shape" if "[go.shape." in name else "Stream"
            return f"Resource[{shape}].{match[1]}{match[2]}"
    if name.startswith(RT + ".NewResource[go.shape.") and STREAM + ".Stream" in name:
        end = name.rfind("]")
        return "NewResource[shape]" + name[end + 1:]
    return None


def regexp_quote(name):
    # Go regexp quoting differs from Python re.escape for spaces in shapes.
    return re.sub(r"([\\.^$|?*+()[\]{}])", r"\\\1", name)


def read_binary(label, binary, tool_dir, out):
    nm = capture(str(tool_dir / "nm"), "-size", str(binary))
    (out / f"{label}.nm.txt").write_text(nm)
    symbols = {}
    selected = {}
    for line in nm.splitlines():
        match = re.match(r"^\s*([0-9a-f]+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\S)\s+(.*)$", line)
        if not match or match[3] != "T":
            continue
        name = match[4]
        key = role(name)
        if key is not None:
            assert key not in selected, (key, selected.get(key), name)
            selected[key] = name
            symbols[name] = {"address": int(match[1], 16), "bytes": int(match[2])}
    assert selected, f"No requested symbols found in {binary}"
    pattern = "^(" + "|".join(regexp_quote(name) for name in selected.values()) + ")$"
    raw = capture(str(tool_dir / "objdump"), "-s", pattern, str(binary))
    (out / f"{label}.raw.txt").write_text(raw)
    bodies = {}
    current = None
    for line in raw.splitlines():
        if line.startswith("TEXT "):
            match = re.match(r"^TEXT (.*)\(SB\) .*", line)
            assert match, line
            current = match[1]
            assert current in symbols, current
            bodies[current] = []
        elif line.strip():
            match = INSN.match(line)
            assert match and current, line
            bodies[current].append((int(match[1], 16), match[3]))
    assert set(bodies) == set(symbols), set(symbols) - set(bodies)
    normalized = {}
    for key, name in selected.items():
        body = bodies[name]
        addresses = {address: index for index, (address, _) in enumerate(body)}
        instructions = []
        for _, assembly in body:
            assembly = re.sub(r"-?0x[0-9a-f]+\(IP\)", "<data>(IP)", assembly)
            mnemonic, _, operands = assembly.partition(" ")
            if mnemonic.startswith("J") or mnemonic in {"CALL", "LOOP", "LOOPE", "LOOPNE"}:
                direct = re.fullmatch(r"0x([0-9a-f]+)", operands.strip())
                if direct:
                    target = int(direct[1], 16)
                    assert target in addresses, (name, assembly)
                    assembly = f"{mnemonic} @I{addresses[target]:04d}"
            instructions.append(" ".join(assembly.split()))
        normalized[key] = {
            "symbol": name,
            **symbols[name],
            "instructions": instructions,
        }
    text = []
    for key in sorted(normalized):
        item = normalized[key]
        text.append(f"FUNCTION {key} bytes={item['bytes']} instructions={len(item['instructions'])}")
        text.extend(f"I{index:04d} {line}" for index, line in enumerate(item["instructions"]))
        text.append("")
    (out / f"{label}.normalized.txt").write_text("\n".join(text))
    return {
        "path": str(binary),
        "sha256": sha256(binary),
        "build_info": capture("/usr/bin/go", "version", "-m", str(binary)),
        "functions": normalized,
    }


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--left", type=Path, default=Path(".cache/sessions-phase0/stream-baseline-r12.test"))
    parser.add_argument("--right", type=Path, default=Path(".cache/sessions-phase0/stream-r12.test"))
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, default=Path(".cache/default-streaming/assembly-frozen"))
    parser.add_argument("--tool-dir", type=Path, default=Path("/home/dario/sdk/go1.24.1/pkg/tool/linux_amd64"))
    args = parser.parse_args()
    args.out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    left = read_binary("left", args.left.resolve(), args.tool_dir, args.out)
    right = read_binary("right", args.right.resolve(), args.tool_dir, args.out)
    result = {
        "utc": datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "command": [sys.executable, *sys.argv],
        "script_sha256": sha256(Path(__file__)),
        "tools": {name: {"path": str(args.tool_dir / name), "sha256": sha256(args.tool_dir / name)}
                  for name in ["nm", "objdump"]},
        "normalization": __doc__,
        "left": left,
        "right": right,
    }
    lines = [
        f"UTC {result['utc']}",
        f"Script SHA256 {result['script_sha256']}",
        f"Left {left['path']} SHA256 {left['sha256']}",
        f"Right {right['path']} SHA256 {right['sha256']}",
        "Only decoding existing binaries; no builds, tests, or benchmarks.",
        "",
        "nm symbol bytes and normalized instruction counts (left -> right):",
    ]
    for key in sorted(left["functions"].keys() | right["functions"].keys()):
        lhs, rhs = left["functions"].get(key), right["functions"].get(key)
        count = lambda item: f"{item['bytes']}B/{len(item['instructions'])}insns" if item else "ABSENT"
        same = bool(lhs and rhs and lhs["bytes"] == rhs["bytes"] and lhs["instructions"] == rhs["instructions"])
        lines.append(f"{key}: {count(lhs)} -> {count(rhs)} {'EQUAL' if same else 'DIFFERENT'}")
        if lhs and rhs and not same:
            lines.extend(difflib.unified_diff(lhs["instructions"], rhs["instructions"],
                                             fromfile=f"left/{key}", tofile=f"right/{key}", lineterm=""))
    lines.extend(["", "Limit: unnamed data relocation targets are masked; raw dumps retain them.",
                  "Equal normalized bodies do not establish causality or performance equivalence."])
    (args.out / "summary.json").write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2) + "\n")
    report = "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
    (args.out / "comparison.txt").write_text(report)
    print(report, end="")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== Diagnostic source preparation and exact builds =====

from pathlib import Path
import os, shutil, subprocess
root=Path('/home/dario/Code/cocoon')
base=Path('/tmp/cocoon-sessions-phase0-sy_birrp')
# The previously generated baseline uses the original Phase0 CLI and the same
# authored streaming fixture, with host-only checked_batches omitted. Its Wasm,
# translated module and corrected standalone harness hashes match current.
source=Path('testdata/streaming/go/streaming/phase_bench_test.go')
shutil.copyfile(root/source,base/source)
env=dict(os.environ,CGO_ENABLED='0',GOTOOLCHAIN='local',GOAMD64='v1',
         GOFLAGS='-buildvcs=false',GOCACHE=str(root/'.cache/go'))
for label,tree in [('baseline',base),('merged',root)]:
    subprocess.run(['/usr/lib64/go/1.26/bin/go','test','-pgo=off','-c','-o',
                    str(root/'.cache/default-streaming'/('phase-'+label+'.test')),
                    './testdata/streaming/go/streaming'],cwd=tree,env=env,check=True)

===== Remove only diagnostic source and reproduce frozen binaries =====

from pathlib import Path
import os, subprocess, hashlib
root=Path('/home/dario/Code/cocoon'); out=root/'.cache/default-streaming'
base=Path('/tmp/cocoon-sessions-phase0-sy_birrp')
env=dict(os.environ,CGO_ENABLED='0',GOTOOLCHAIN='local',GOAMD64='v1',
         GOFLAGS='-buildvcs=false',GOCACHE=str(root/'.cache/go'))
for label,tree,original in [('baseline',base,root/'.cache/sessions-phase0/stream-baseline-r12.test'),
                            ('merged',root,root/'.cache/sessions-phase0/stream-r12.test')]:
    source=tree/'testdata/streaming/go/streaming/phase_bench_test.go'
    saved=source.with_name('.phase_bench_test.go')
    source.rename(saved) # Go ignores the dot-prefixed file; same-directory rename.
    try:
        binary=out/('rebuilt-'+label+'.test')
        subprocess.run(['/usr/lib64/go/1.26/bin/go','test','-pgo=off','-c','-o',
                        str(binary),'./testdata/streaming/go/streaming'],
                       cwd=tree,env=env,check=True)
        sha=lambda path: hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
        print(label,'frozen',sha(original),'rebuilt',sha(binary),
              'identical',binary.read_bytes()==original.read_bytes())
    finally:
        saved.rename(source)

===== Proof reproduction driver =====

import os, hashlib, json, subprocess
from pathlib import Path
root = Path.cwd()
out = root / '.cache/default-streaming'
env = dict(os.environ, GOTOOLCHAIN='local', CGO_ENABLED='0', GOCACHE=str(root/'.cache/go'), GOFLAGS='-buildvcs=false')
env['PATH'] = str(root/'.cache/binaryen/binaryen-version_133/bin')+':'+env['PATH']
roots = ['testdata/compute', 'examples/datadog', 'testdata/streaming', 'rt/instance.go']
paths = subprocess.check_output(['git','ls-files','--',*roots],text=True).splitlines()
def fingerprints():
    return {p:hashlib.sha256((root/p).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in paths}
expected = fingerprints()
(out/'proof-reproduce-before.json').write_text(json.dumps(expected,indent=2)+'\n')
with (out/'proof-reproduce.log').open('w') as log:
    for number in (1,2):
        for manifest in ['testdata/compute/cocoon.toml','examples/datadog/cocoon.toml','testdata/streaming/cocoon.toml']:
            args=['/usr/bin/go','run','./cmd/cocoon','build','--manifest',manifest]
            print('Repeat',number,' '.join(args),file=log,flush=True)
            subprocess.run(args,env=env,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,check=True)
        subprocess.run(['/usr/bin/go','generate','./rt'],env=env,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,check=True)
        actual=fingerprints()
        assert actual == expected, {p:(expected.get(p),actual.get(p)) for p in paths if expected.get(p)!=actual.get(p)}
        subprocess.run(['git','diff','--exit-code','HEAD','--',*roots],stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,check=True)
        print('PASS: all',len(paths),'committed proof/runtime files reproduce byte-for-byte in repeat',number,file=log,flush=True)
(out/'proof-reproduce-after.json').write_text(json.dumps(fingerprints(),indent=2)+'\n')
print('PASS: two repeated builds of all three proofs and runtime adapters; '+str(len(paths))+' files unchanged',flush=True)

Proof fingerprint maps byte-identical, SHA256 fb6520a18c81a9d8c70990dbcb7b705e9003614dc4ad5f20fc6659dd083fae4b
